     - R → G → B → T → F → S → E → A → S → B → C → J → I
     - R → G → B → T → F → E → S → A → S → B → C → J → I
     - R → G → B → T → F → S → E → A → B → C → J → I
     - R → O → B → C → J → I

   - Additionally, breaking the path through node B might also be necessary since it connects several paths between R and I.

   - Path 2: Q → G → B → H → N

   - Path 2 goes through G and B.
   - Path 3 goes through G and B.

   - To ensure all paths are disconnected, we need to remove both I and either G or B.

   - Removing both I and G or removing both I and B will disconnect all paths between Q and N.
